Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the electronics object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 329. The moduli space has dimension at most 615 with 5 connected components.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to electronics structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 329/615 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.
